NEWCHWANG. 3 (Received 23rd July 1906.) CUSTOM HOUSE, NEWCHWANG, i6th July 1906. V. BRENAN, Acting Commisnionerof atoms. TO the INSPECTOR GENERAL OF CUSTOMR.
1906.] kiaochow. 287 (Received 30th July 1906j CUSTOM HOUSE, KIAOCHOW, 24th July 1906. SIR, I.&mdash;a. In conformity with the instructions contained in your Circulars Nos. 21 of 1869, 14 of 1872, and 6 of 1875, I now transmit to the Statistical Department at Shanghai, for publication, the statistics of the trade of this port for the second quarter of the year 1906. b. The Provisional Customs Arrangement between China and Germany, under which the Kiaochow Custom House was opened at Tsingtau on the 1st July 1899, was amended by the additional Convention of the 1st December 1905. Under the latter Agreement the Free area, which until then comprised the whole Pachtgebiet, was on the 1st January 1906 restricted to the Harbour territory. Goods arriving are stored and re-exported from there free of Duty. On leaving the Free port area Imports and Exports pay the same Duties as at the Treaty Ports. II. TONNAGE.&mdash;The number, tonnage, and movements of vessels, entered and cleared during the quarter at the German port of Tsingtau, are set forth in the following Table:&mdash; ENTERED. April. May.
